This analysis reveals that the required evidence is present in the vast majority of failures: the bottleneck is not data access but the agent's ability to reason over it correctly. We further introduce an automated rule mining pipeline that systematically extracts discrimination rules from reverse reasoning reports, reducing reliance on manual knowledge curation. Across all configurations, model reasoning capability and domain knowledge are the primary constraints: stronger models embed more domain expertise, and explicit knowledge injection partially compensates for this gap. Reasoning performance remains practically bounded even when evidence extraction is perfect: scaffold engineering and better data pipelines alone cannot close this gap; progress requires improvements at the model level.

</details>


### [140] [Multi-Agent Collaborative Reasoning with Tool-Augmented Evidence for Urban Region Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13558)
*Xixuan Hao,Yutian Jiang,Jiabo Liu,Yihang Yang,Guangyin Jin,Song Gao,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UrbanAgent 将城市区域画像从传统多模态表征学习转化为推理驱动的多智能体协作推断，通过主动获取证据和迭代验证提升碳排放、GDP、人口等指标预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市区域画像方法多依赖异构数据融合与相关性学习，假设跨模态信息一致，并采用静态流程；在异质或未见城市区域中容易受跨模态不一致和泛化不足影响。

Method: 提出 UrbanAgent：为每种数据模态构建独立智能体，进行结构化多智能体协作推理，显式处理跨模态不一致；同时将指标预测扩展为闭环过程，通过工具增强的外部知识检索、主动证据获取与迭代推理来验证不确定推断，并使用强化学习优化该过程。

Result: 在全球城市数据集上针对碳排放、GDP 和人口估计进行实验，UrbanAgent 相比现有基线持续取得更好表现，R2 平均提升 8.1%，并在未见城市设置下表现出较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，面向城市区域画像的推理式、多智能体和闭环证据获取框架比静态多模态融合更鲁棒，尤其适用于跨区域、跨模态异质性较强的预测任务。

Abstract: Urban region profiling constitutes a core problem in urban computing, supporting applications such as population estimation, economic assessment, and environmental monitoring. Existing methods typically formulate this task as multimodal representation learning, fusing heterogeneous urban data, e.g., satellite imagery, points of interest, textual descriptions, and 3D building information, into latent embeddings for prediction. However, these approaches are largely correlation-driven, assume cross-modal consistency, and rely on static pipelines, which limit their robustness in heterogeneous or unseen urban regions. We propose UrbanAgent, an agentic framework that reframes urban region profiling as a reasoning-driven inference problem. UrbanAgent instantiates an independent agent for each data modality and performs structured multi-agent collaborative reasoning to explicitly address cross-modal inconsistencies rather than absorbing them into a single representation. In addition, UrbanAgent extends indicator prediction as a closed-loop process of active evidence acquisition and iterative reasoning, enabling agents to verify uncertain inferences through tool-augmented retrieval of external knowledge optimized via reinforcement learning. Extensive experiments on global urban datasets for Carbon emissions, GDP, and Population estimation show that UrbanAgent consistently outperforms existing baselines, achieving an average improvement of 8.1% in R2, and exhibiting strong generalization performance in unseen-city settings.

</details>


### [141] [AI advice suppresses people's willingness to say "I don't know", even when the advice is wrong and accuracy is incentivized](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13562)
*Chiara Marcoccia,Walter Quattrociocchi,Valerio Capraro*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究表明：即使在AI建议被设计为错误的情况下，仅仅能够接触到AI建议，也会显著降低人们选择“我不知道/不作答”的意愿，并提升错误作答与过度自信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类判断中，知道何时承认“不知道”非常重要；但AI助手往往会对几乎任何问题给出流畅答案。论文关注AI建议是否会改变人们的元认知判断阈值，即人们在不确定时是否仍愿意暂停判断。

Method: 研究通过五个实验（总样本N=3,132，其中四个预注册、一个直接复现）让参与者回答困难问题，并始终允许其拒答。研究者特意设计问题，使AI建议为错误答案，从而将“使用AI”的影响与“AI准确性”的影响分离；同时比较无AI、主动请求AI、自动显示AI建议，以及引入准确性激励和错误惩罚等条件。

Result: AI可用几乎消除了参与者暂停判断的倾向，无论AI建议是主动请求还是自动展示。参与者回答的问题更多，但正确率约为无AI条件下的三分之一，同时信心几乎翻倍。准确性激励和错误惩罚会减少参与者寻求和采纳AI建议，提高准确率，并增加拒答，但拒答水平仍远低于无AI条件。

Conclusion: AI建议不仅影响答案是否正确，还可能改变人们判断自己是否“知道得足够多以便作答”的元认知阈值。随着AI建议变得普遍且可能未经请求地出现，其风险不只是误导答案，更在于削弱人们承认不确定性的能力。

Abstract: Knowing when to say "I don't know" is fundamental to human judgment, yet AI assistants offer a fluent answer to almost any question. In five experiments (N = 3,132; four preregistered, one direct replication), participants answered difficult questions and could always decline to respond. We engineered the questions so that AI advice was wrong, separating AI use from its accuracy. Merely having access to AI nearly eliminated participants' willingness to suspend judgment, and this held whether the advice was actively requested or simply displayed. Consequently, participants answered more questions but were correct about a third as often as when AI was unavailable-yet their confidence nearly doubled. Incentivizing accuracy and penalizing inaccuracy led participants to seek and follow AI advice less, answer more accurately, and suspend judgment more often, though still far less than when AI was unavailable. As AI suggestions grow ubiquitous and unsolicited, they may not simply affect answer accuracy; they may even alter the metacognitive threshold at which people decide whether they know enough to answer.

</details>


### [142] [SAFETY SENTRY: Context-Aware Human Intervention via EXECUTE-ASK-REFUSE Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13594)
*Tianyu Chen,Chujia Hu,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出将LLM智能体工具调用的安全防护从二分类改为逐实例三路由决策：执行、询问或拒绝，并用轻量级模型Safety Sentry实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有守护模型通常只判断动作安全/不安全，混淆了动作本身是否有害与在用户上下文中是否合适两类问题；同时按动作类别而非具体实例拦截，容易造成频繁打断、降低用户自主性并使用户忽视重要警报。

Method: 作者将问题重构为每个动作实例上的{EXECUTE, ASK, REFUSE}三分类/路由任务，提出Safety Sentry；其推理过程只需一次解码调用，并通过单一解码时阈值在不重新训练的情况下调节同一检查点以适配不同风险偏好的部署场景。

Result: Safety Sentry在总体准确率和安全相关召回率上优于多种开源权重模型与前沿闭源基线，同时能够控制两类方向性错误率。

Conclusion: 逐实例三路由机制比传统二元安全判定更细粒度、更符合实际部署需求；Safety Sentry提供了一种轻量、可调且性能更优的LLM智能体工具调用安全防护方案。

Abstract: LLM agents act on real-world environments through tool calls, and a single misjudged action can cause irreversible harm. The standard safeguard is a guard model that labels each proposed action as safe or unsafe, but this binary view conflates two distinct decisions: whether the action is harmful in itself, and whether it is appropriate given the user's context. It also operates at the granularity of action categories rather than individual instances, producing routine interruptions that erode autonomy and train users to wave through the most consequential alerts. We reframe the problem as a per-instance three-way routing decision over {EXECUTE, ASK, REFUSE} and instantiate it with Safety Sentry, a lightweight guard model whose inference reduces to a single decoding call. A single decoding-time threshold lets one fixed checkpoint be re-positioned across deployments of differing risk tolerance without retraining. Safety Sentry outperforms a broad set of open-weight and frontier closed-source baselines on overall accuracy and safety-related recall, while controlling both directional error rates simultaneously.

</details>


### [143] [Automatic Ordinary Differential Equations Discovery For Biological Systems Using Large Language Model Powered Agentic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13608)
*David Krongauz,Arad Zulti,Eran Segal,Teddy Lazebnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MEDA，一个结合大语言模型代理与符号回归的框架，用于自动发现生物动力系统的ODE机制模型，并在检索、外推和开放式发现任务中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动科学发现方法多集中于狭窄的方程发现基准或宽泛的自动化流程，对生物系统中的机制性ODE模型发现探索不足；同时，仅依赖数值拟合可能得到轨迹相符但机制错误的模型。

Method: MEDA通过检索背景知识、定义可接受变量、生成机制约束、提出候选ODE，并进行拟合与评估；结合LLM的知识推理能力和符号回归的方程搜索能力，在有无实验数据条件下进行模型发现。

Result: 在经典模型检索、对未见变体的推理外推和开放式发现任务中，MEDA能够恢复正确状态变量，在检索与外推任务中取得较强结构恢复效果，并生成具有生物合理性的发现型模型。消融与鲁棒性分析表明，知识引导形式化和机制约束是关键组件。

Conclusion: LLM与符号回归结合的知识引导代理框架可提升生物ODE模型自动发现的机制正确性；相比单纯数值拟合，MEDA更能避免生物学上不合理但轨迹拟合良好的方程。

Abstract: Automatic scientific discovery has long been a goal of computational scholars - a machine that can discover nature's secrets on its own, moving computational systems beyond data-fitting tools toward the generation and refinement of mechanistic models of the universe. Recent advances in symbolic regression (SR) and large-language-model (LLM)-based agents suggest that such systems can recover equations from data, incorporate domain priors, and automate parts of the research workflow. However, most existing approaches either focus on narrow equation-discovery benchmarks or broad end-to-end automation pipelines, while biological systems remain comparatively underexplored. Here, we introduce the MEDA system, an LLM- and SR-powered agentic framework for discovering ordinary-differential-equation (ODE) models of biological and biologically inspired dynamical systems. MEDA retrieves background knowledge, defines admissible variables, generates mechanistic constraints, proposes candidate ODEs, and fits and evaluates them. We evaluate it across canonical model retrieval, reasoning-based extrapolation to unseen variants, and open-ended discovery, with and without experimental data. Across these settings, MEDA recovered the correct state variables, achieved strong structural recovery in retrieval and extrapolation tasks, and produced biologically plausible discovery-oriented models. Ablation and robustness analyses show that knowledge-guided formalization and mechanistic constraints are load-bearing components, whereas numerical fitting alone can preserve trajectory-compatible but biologically incorrect equations.

</details>


### [144] [STOCKTAKE: Measuring the Gap Between Perception and Action in LLM Agents with a Fair Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13618)
*Sagar Deb,Ashwanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 STOCKTAKE：一个用于评估 LLM 智能体在部分可观测、多周供应链补货任务中表现的基准。它不仅衡量最终成本/技能分数，还区分“是否正确识别隐藏状态”和“识别后是否采取有效行动”的 knowing-doing gap。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期决策任务评估通常只看最终成本，无法判断智能体失败是因为误读环境状态，还是已经理解状态但控制决策失败。同时，既有参考策略要么依赖智能体不可见的特权信息，要么缺少公平可比的参考基线。

Method: 作者构建了一个 26 周供应链补货基准 STOCKTAKE，将任务建模为因子化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程，包含 6 个隐藏因子过程。基准使用与智能体相同观测流的精确贝叶斯滤波器，为每个因子估计隐状态，并驱动 rollout 参考策略。评分方面，将每次运行归一化到“症状盲 base-stock floor”为 0、“贝叶斯 oracle”为 1 的技能分数；同时对每周书面理由进行评分，得到 stated-belief detection lag 与 knowing-doing rate，从而分别衡量状态估计与控制能力。

Result: 在 50 个随机种子和人工设计压力情景下，Claude Sonnet 5、GPT-5.4、DeepSeek-V4-Pro、Grok 4.5 能检测到 84–88% 的隐藏故障，通常在故障发生后一周内识别。然而其技能分数差异很大，从 0.62 到 -0.23；其中两个模型低于症状盲基线，尽管它们识别隐藏因素的速度略快于表现更好的两个模型。在持续压力周中，即使模型已正确诊断压力，仍有 34–43% 的周发生缺货。

Conclusion: STOCKTAKE 表明，LLM 智能体的长期决策失败不能仅归因于状态识别错误；即使正确识别隐藏压力，模型也可能响应不足，或采取成本过高的过度响应。该基准能同时测量状态估计能力与控制决策质量，并揭示 knowing-doing gap 的双向失败模式。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly evaluated on multi-week decision tasks in which the state that drives cost is never directly observed. On such tasks the final cost cannot say why an agent failed: it may have misread the world, or read it correctly and still failed to act (the knowing-doing gap). Existing evaluations cannot separate these two failures; their reference policies either read privileged information the agent never sees, or are missing altogether. We introduce STOCKTAKE, a 26-week supply-chain replenishment benchmark built as a factored partially observable Markov decision process with six hidden factor processes, designed so that a fair reference policy is computable: an exact Bayes filter per factor drives a rollout policy on the identical observation stream the agent receives. Scoring each run between a symptom-blind base-stock floor (0) and this oracle (1) yields a skill score, and grading each week's written rationale yields a stated-belief detection lag and a knowing-doing rate, so state estimation and control are measured separately. On fifty seeds with curated stress profiles, Claude Sonnet 5, GPT-5.4, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, and Grok 4.5 detect 84-88% of hidden failures, typically within a week of onset, yet span skill scores from 0.62 to -0.23: two of the four end below the symptom-blind floor while naming factors slightly faster than the two that beat it. The failure has two faces. Where stress persists, 34-43% of correctly diagnosed stress weeks still end in stockout for every model, a rate that partly reflects the severity of the weeks models notice. That rate also runs opposite to skill: the two models under the floor stock out least on diagnosed weeks, so under-response is only one face of the gap, and their traces point to the other, responses whose cost exceeds what they protect. STOCKTAKE measures both directions of that failure.

</details>


### [145] [UESF-Bench: Benchmarking and Probing for Unified Embodied Seeking and Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13621)
*Kun Yu,Jianhua Yang,Yixiang Chen,Changwei Wang,Hongyuan Yu,Yan Huang,Fushuo Huo,Ya Jing,Zhumin Chen,Keji He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 UESF-Bench，用于统一评估具身智能体在语言引导下先寻找目标人物再持续跟随的能力，并提出 SeekFollow-VLA 框架，在单人和多人环境中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言引导跟随基准通常假设目标一开始可见，降低了任务难度；已有人物搜索研究又多依赖特定场景或较强环境先验，并将搜索与跟随割裂，缺乏统一系统评估。

Method: 构建大规模多样化的 Unified Embodied Seeking and Following Benchmark（UESF-Bench），要求智能体完成语义引导探索、搜索/跟随行为切换与恢复、延迟身份定位；同时提出 SeekFollow-VLA，一种视觉-语言-动作框架，通过任务驱动路由机制进行潜在阶段推断和搜索-跟随转换建模。

Result: 实验表明，SeekFollow-VLA 在单人和多人环境中均显著优于单头和双头基线方法。

Conclusion: UESF-Bench 为统一的具身人物寻找与跟随任务提供了系统评测平台，SeekFollow-VLA 为该任务建立了有效基线，证明显式建模阶段切换和任务路由对复杂动态环境中的 seek-and-follow 能力很重要。

Abstract: Language-guided human following is an important capability for embodied agents, but existing benchmarks typically assume that the target person is visible at the start of an episode. This setting simplifies the problem and overlooks a more realistic requirement: an agent often needs to first find a language-described target and then persistently follow that target in a dynamic environment. While recent work has started to study human search, existing settings are typically evaluated in task-specific scenarios and often rely on stronger prior knowledge of the environment. Moreover, they usually treat searching and following as separate tasks and still lack a unified benchmark for systematic evaluation. To address these limitations, we introduce the Unified Embodied Seeking and Following Benchmark (UESF-Bench), a large-scale and diverse benchmark for embodied human seeking and following. The benchmark requires agents to handle semantic-guided exploration, reliable behavior switching and recovery, and delayed identity grounding. To this end, we propose SeekFollow-VLA, a vision-language-action framework with a task-driven routing mechanism for latent phase inference and transition modeling between seeking and following. Experimental results show that SeekFollow-VLA achieves clear improvements over both single-head and dual-head baselines across single-person and multi-person environments, establishing a baseline for unified embodied seek-and-follow.

</details>


### [146] [Explaining Reinforcement Learning Agents via Inductive Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13655)
*Celeste Veronese,Edoardo Zorzi,Daniele Meli,Alessandro Farinelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向强化学习策略可解释性的客观、规划导向指标，并用归纳逻辑编程抽取符号规则来量化解释质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释强化学习多依赖用户研究，缺乏通用客观评价指标；逻辑型XAI虽能给出紧凑可读的规则表示，但如何系统量化逻辑规则的可解释程度仍未解决。

Method: 采用归纳逻辑编程从强化学习策略中提取符号化逻辑规则，并定义 activation rate、feature coverage、syntactic distance、semantic distance 等指标，用于衡量规则与智能体行为的一致性、特征在决策中的作用，以及单智能体/多智能体训练过程中的策略演化。

Result: 在多个强化学习领域的实验表明，这些指标能揭示超越全局回报的动作级学习动态，提供比传统全局特征重要性方法更细粒度的领域特征洞察，并发现多智能体强化学习中的协作、专门化与适应模式。

Conclusion: 该工作为XRL提供了客观、可比较的解释性评估框架，同时推动逻辑XAI从直觉性评估走向原则化量化，有助于理解策略迁移、泛化与动作特定策略的演化。

Abstract: Explainable Reinforcement Learning (XRL) seeks to make Reinforcement Learning (RL) policies more transparent and interpretable, a key requirement in safety-critical and human-centric scenarios. However, it is mostly based on user studies, thus targeting the needs of a specific audience and lacking shared evaluation metrics. On the other hand, logic-based approaches within eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) provide compact, human-readable abstractions of decision-making. However, the systematic quantification of the explainability degree of logical representations remains an open problem. This work aims to advance the state of the art in XRL by introducing objective and planning-oriented metrics for policy explainability in RL settings. At the same time, it contributes to the field of logic for XAI by providing a principled way to quantify the explainability of logical rules, moving beyond common-sense assessments and simple propositional fragments. We employ Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) to extract symbolic representations of RL policies and define a novel set of explainability metrics, including activation rate, feature coverage, syntactic distance and semantic distance. These metrics quantify alignment between symbolic rules and agent behavior, the role of features in decision-making, and the evolution of policies during training and across agents in single and multi-agent RL. Experiments across different RL domains show that the proposed metrics highlight action-specific learning dynamics beyond global return, provide fine-grained insights into domain features beyond classical approaches for global feature importance estimation, and uncover coordination, specialization, and adaptation patterns in MARL. Moreover, they provide crucial insights for the transfer and generalization of action-specific policies.

</details>


### [147] [When Bots Join the Team: Bot Adoption and the Institutional Fabric of Open-Source Software Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13679)
*Yongren Shi,Wenyi Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究开源软件项目中机器人（bot）首次加入后，团队组织能力与产出变化的关联。结果显示，bot 采用后重复协作、社会记忆和角色分化增强，冲突级联减少，产出更具独特性；但由于缺少未采用 bot 的对照组，结论应理解为时间上精确的关联而非因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 代理进入人类团队，核心问题是：自动化代理成为常规参与者后，会增强还是削弱群体组织？作者选择 GitHub 开源项目作为场景，因为 bot 与人类共同提交、审查和合并代码，并留下完整互动记录，适合观察组织结构变化。

Method: 作者将 bot 视为团队参与者而非单纯工具，分析 2,991 个 GitHub 项目在首次采用 bot 前后各两年的数据。研究测量三类与持久协作相关的制度能力：重复参与、社会记忆、角色分化；并考察两个结果变量：冲突级联和输出独特性。分析重点是 bot 采用前后变化的时间聚集性及能力变量与结果之间的对应关系。

Result: bot 采用后，项目中重复协作增加，讨论中对特定 bot 的识别增强，冲突级联减少，输出更具独特性。这些变化集中出现在采用 bot 附近，而非随时间缓慢累积。进一步模式显示，能力对结果的预测取决于其组织功能（协调或分化），而不是能力由人类还是 bot 提供；人类侧能力可以解释 bot 与冲突减少之间的关联，但不能解释 bot 与输出独特性之间的关联。

Conclusion: 研究认为，规则明确、行为可预测的自动化代理能够成为社区社会基础设施的一部分。bot 本身不是唯一机制，它更像是触发组织重构的契机；真正发挥作用的是团队社会组织能力的变化。

Abstract: AI agents are joining human teams, raising a basic question: when an automated agent becomes a regular participant, does group organization strengthen or weaken? We study this question in open-source software, where bots open pull requests, review code, and merge changes alongside people, leaving a public record of every interaction. Treating bots as participants rather than tools, we examine 2,991 GitHub projects for two years before and after each adopted its first bot. We measure three capabilities that institutional theory links to durable coordination - repeated engagement, social memory, and role differentiation - and two outcomes: conflict cascades and output distinctiveness. Bot adoption is followed by more repeated collaboration, greater recognition of specific bots in discussion, fewer conflict cascades, and more distinctive outputs. These changes cluster around adoption rather than accumulating gradually. Because we lack an untreated comparison group, we interpret the results as precisely timed associations, not causal effects. Two patterns are difficult for alternative explanations to account for: capabilities predict outcomes according to their function - coordination versus differentiation - rather than whether humans or bots provide them, and human-side capabilities account for the bot-conflict association but not the bot-distinctiveness association. The findings are consistent with a specific interpretation: predictable, rule-based agents can become part of a community's social infrastructure. The bot is the occasion; social organization is the mechanism.

</details>


### [148] [AgentCompass: A Unified Evaluation Infrastructure for Agent Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13705)
*Zichen Ding,Jiaye Ge,Shufan Jiang,Kai Chen,Mo Li,Qingqiu Li,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tiaohao Liang,Shudong Liu,Zerun Ma,Zixing Shang,Wenhui Tian,Zun Wang,Liwei Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Jun Xu,Bowen Yang,Dingbo Yuan,Qi Zhang,Songyang Zhang,Peiheng Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentCompass 是一个面向 LLM 智能体评测的开源、轻量、可扩展基础设施，旨在提升评测流程的统一性、可复现性和诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型发展为自主智能体，研究社区需要统一的评测基础设施；现有评测流程碎片化、组件强耦合，导致复现困难、重复工程成本高，并限制了灵活配置与系统化分析。

Method: 论文提出 AgentCompass，将评测过程解耦为三个独立组件：Benchmark、Harness 和 Environment，使用户无需重写复杂执行逻辑即可灵活组合配置。同时引入容错异步运行时，并提供轨迹分析工具，用于透明诊断智能体执行过程中的细粒度失败模式。

Result: AgentCompass 原生支持超过 20 个基准测试，覆盖五类能力维度；其异步与容错机制支持更稳定的大规模评测，轨迹分析可帮助识别如 reward-hacking 等复杂问题。

Conclusion: AgentCompass 为 LLM 智能体研究提供了可扩展、可复现且易扩展的统一评测基础设施，有助于减少重复开发并推动智能体评测标准化。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous agents, the need for unified evaluation infrastructure becomes critical. However, current evaluation pipelines remain highly fragmented and tightly coupled, hindering reproducibility and causing redundant engineering. To address this, we introduce AgentCompass, an open-source, lightweight, and extensible infrastructure for evaluating LLM-based agents. AgentCompass organizes the evaluation process around three independent components, namely Benchmark, Harness, and Environment, thereby enabling flexible configurations without requiring the reimplementation of complex execution logic. Furthermore, it features a fault-tolerant asynchronous runtime and comprehensive trajectory analysis tools to transparently diagnose nuanced failure modes like reward-hacking. Natively supporting over 20 benchmarks across five capability dimensions, AgentCompass provides the community with a scalable and reproducible infrastructure for advancing agent research.

</details>


### [149] [CAVA: Canonical Action Verification and Attestation for Runtime Governance of Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13716)
*Zexun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 CAVA，将异构运行时中的智能体操作转换为可验证、可复现、可治理的规范化动作对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体 AI 系统在多种异构运行时中执行同一类操作，导致审批内容、执行证据和后续独立验证之间难以建立一致绑定，影响部署方治理。

Method: 提出 Canonical Action Verification and Attestation（CAVA）作为运行时语义层，形式化规范动作身份、语义模式检测、审批绑定、回执完整性、跨运行时投影和可选证明基底，并将其定位为 PCAA 治理流程之下的稳定动作对象层。

Result: 通过包含 96 个种子、384 个变体的基准测试，覆盖语义等价/分离、包装绕过、误报控制、审批绑定、回执复现、证明篡改检测、运行时可移植性、语义模式检测、策略退化和 Azure 部署演练等场景，验证参考实现。

Conclusion: CAVA 为动作级规范化和可由策略寻址的语义模式提供系统化方案，是部署方 AI 治理中连接审批、执行证据和独立验证的必要基础设施。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly act through heterogeneous runtimes: local coding hooks, SDK tools, browser automation, managed-agent traces, API gateways, and workflow engines. A single operational act such as publishing code, changing identity state, moving money, or exporting data may therefore be represented by many incompatible runtime records. This makes a basic governance question difficult to answer: what action was actually approved, what evidence binds the approval to execution, and can an independent verifier reproduce the same action identity later?
  This paper presents Canonical Action Verification and Attestation (CAVA), a runtime-semantics layer for converting heterogeneous agent activity into canonical runtime action objects. CAVA is positioned below Proof-Carrying Agent Actions (PCAA): PCAA defines the deployer-owned route-review-prove governance process, while CAVA defines the stable action object that process governs. The paper formalizes canonical action identity, semantic pattern detection, approval binding, receipt integrity, runtime-portable projection, and optional attestation substrates. We study a reference implementation through a 96-seed, 384-variant benchmark covering semantic equivalence, semantic separation, wrapper bypass, false-positive control, approval binding, receipt reproducibility, attestation tamper detection, runtime portability, semantic pattern detection, policy degradation, and Azure deployment drills. The contribution is a systems formulation of action-level canonicalization and policy-addressable semantic patterns as a necessary substrate for deployer-side AI governance.

</details>


### [150] [Experience Memory Graph: One-Shot Error Correction for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13884)
*Wenjun Wang,Yuchen Fang,Fengrui Liu,Zibo Liang,Kai Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Experience Memory Graph（EMG），将 LLM 智能体的失败恢复建模为图匹配问题，通过从失败轨迹与专家成功轨迹中提取可复用的成功工作流和纠错编辑路径，在测试时实现无需反复试错的单次执行指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体在复杂长程任务中容易出现累积错误且难以恢复；现有基于提示的自我反思方法脆弱、耗时、API 成本高，并且生成的任务特定记忆泛化能力有限。

Method: 训练阶段将失败探索轨迹和成功专家轨迹转换为有向动作决策图；通过图匹配提取公共子图作为成功工作流，并提取图编辑路径以表示在特定观察下应增加、删除或重标记的动作；随后将这些信息存入包含任务内节点和跨任务边的记忆图。测试阶段检索相关经验并指导智能体进行单轮、无循环执行。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 和 ScienceWorld 上，EMG 在成功率和平均奖励方面持续优于先进的反思类基线方法，并且不需要测试时的反复试错。

Conclusion: EMG 通过结构化经验记忆和显式图编辑纠错，将失败恢复从脆弱的提示反思转化为可检索、可泛化的图匹配机制，从而提升长程任务中 LLM 智能体的可靠性和效率。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown remarkable capabilities in autonomous decision-making by generating sequential trajectories of states, actions, and observations. However, in complex, long-horizon tasks, these agents frequently suffer from compounding errors and struggle to recover from failures. Existing self-correction mechanisms rely on prompt-based reflection, which is inherently brittle, incurs heavy time and API costs due to iterative trial-and-error loops, and produces task-specific memory that may be hard to generalize to new scenarios. To address this, we propose Experience Memory Graph (EMG), a framework that reformulates agent failure recovery as a graph matching problem. At training time, we convert both failed exploration trajectories and successful expert trajectories into directed action decision graphs. By matching these graphs, we extract common subgraphs (successful workflows) and graph edit paths that explicitly indicate how to correct failures (e.g., which actions to add, delete, or relabel under a given observation), and store them in a memory graph with intra-task nodes and cross-task edges. At test time, EMG retrieves relevant insights and guides the agent in a single, loop-free execution. Experiments on ALFWorld and ScienceWorld show that EMG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art reflection baselines in success rate and average reward, while requiring no test-time trial-and-error.

</details>


### [151] [AIMO Interpretability Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13899)
*Michal Štefánik,Philipp Mondorf,Andreas Waldis,Qianying Liu,Chuan Yang,Michal Spiegel,Josef Kuchař,Marek Kadlčík,Adam Vawda-Oomerjee,Chaoran Liu,Simon Frieder,Barbara Plank,Fazl Barez,Pontus Stenetorp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AIMO Interpretability Challenge，旨在通过分析前沿数学语言模型的内部机制，区分其数学推理是稳健的还是依赖虚假捷径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理基准主要关注最终答案正确率，但高准确率无法说明模型是否真正使用稳定、可泛化的推理机制，还是利用脆弱的模式匹配或捷径。

Method: 竞赛基于 AIMO 数学问题与提交内容，并结合 Fields Model Initiative 资源，提供新发布的奥赛级数学题、符号化表示、可生成的功能变体、前沿推理模型访问权限，以及对抗鲁棒性评估；参赛者利用这些资源和计算基础设施开发识别模型稳健求解能力的方法。

Result: 预期产出包括新的开放鲁棒性基准、基线系统，以及用于评估数学推理模型可解释性与鲁棒性的标准化资源。

Conclusion: 该竞赛试图将可解释性研究与泛化研究连接起来，核心目标是判断前沿 AI 模型的决策机制是否具有可泛化性与可靠性，从而推动数学推理模型评测从结果导向转向机制导向。

Abstract: We propose the AIMO Interpretability Challenge, a competition on distinguishing robust from spurious reasoning in frontier mathematical language models based on the models' internal mechanisms. The challenge is motivated by a central limitation of standard reasoning benchmarks: strong final-answer accuracy does not reveal whether a model relies on stable reasoning mechanisms or exploits brittle reasoning shortcuts. Building on AI Mathematical Olympiad (AIMO) problems and submissions, together with resources from the Fields Model Initiative, the competition will provide (1) newly-published olympiad-level math reasoning problems and their symbolic representations, allowing generation of novel functional variants, (2) access to frontier reasoning models, and (3) assessments of models' adversarial robustness on these problems. Participants will use these resources, along with our computing infrastructure support, to develop methods for identifying which models solve problems robustly. Our competition will also create a new, open robustness benchmark and baseline systems, aiming to provide a lasting foundation for standard benchmarking in mathematical reasoning and interpretability. Scientifically, the competition connects interpretability and generalization research around a central question in AI research: can we determine if, and to what extent, the decision-making of frontier AI models is generalizable and thus, reliable?

</details>


### [152] [A Self-Evolving Agent for Longitudinal Personal Health Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13940)
*Haoran Li,Jiebi Deng,Tong Jin,Jinghong Han,Yuxin Wang,Zexin Wang,Qingyi Si,Weikang Gong,Xiahai Zhuang,Jia You,Wei Cheng,Jianfeng Feng,Hongcheng Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HealthClaw：一种面向纵向个人健康管理的开源智能体架构，通过受治理的自演化记忆显著提升长期健康支持、隐私保护与生物医学任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有健康 AI 系统通常将每次请求孤立处理，难以适应个人健康管理中随时间变化的日常习惯、偏好、测量数据和风险状态；同时，长期记忆的使用还需兼顾安全、隐私与医学知识治理。

Method: HealthClaw 将共享安全规则和医学知识与私有纵向记忆分离；私有记忆包含个人档案事实、可复用流程和情节性轨迹。每次交互后，系统通过归纳机制判断信息应更新到档案、修订流程、保留为情节记忆或排除。作者使用合成的一年期纵向基准、900 个纵向支持探针、100 个隐私探针以及九个各 200 例的生物医学任务进行离线评估。

Result: 在 900 个纵向支持探针中，HealthClaw 将答案准确率从当前查询提示的 0.2% 提升至 45.7%，同时相较完整历史提示减少 71.7% 的提示端上下文暴露。在 100 个隐私探针中，其隐私感知回答质量更高且不安全披露更少。在九项生物医学任务中，任务特定主要指标平均绝对提升 27.0 个百分点，其中七项在错误发现率校正后仍显著。

Conclusion: 离线基准结果表明，受治理的自演化记忆机制有望支持纵向个人健康智能体，在准确性、隐私保护和任务性能上优于基线；但其真实临床有效性仍需前瞻性研究验证。

Abstract: Personal health management unfolds over repeated encounters, yet most health AI systems treat each request in isolation. We developed HealthClaw, an open-source agent architecture that updates support as a person's routines, preferences, measurements and risks change. It separates shared safety rules and medical knowledge from private longitudinal memory containing profile facts, reusable procedures and episodic traces. After each episode, induction determines what should update the profile, revise a procedure, remain episodic or be excluded. We evaluated HealthClaw with a synthetic year-long benchmark and nine 200-case biomedical tasks. Across 900 longitudinal support probes, answer accuracy increased from 0.2% with current-query prompting to 45.7% with HealthClaw, while prompt-side context exposure was 71.7% lower than with full-history prompting. In 100 privacy probes, HealthClaw produced higher privacy-aware answer quality and fewer unsafe disclosures than both baselines. Across the biomedical tasks, the mean absolute gain in the task-specific primary metric was 27.0 percentage points, and seven gains remained significant after false-discovery-rate correction. These offline benchmarks support governed, self-evolving memory for longitudinal personal health agents, although clinical effectiveness requires prospective evaluation. HealthClaw is publicly available at https://github.com/HC-Guo/HealthClaw.

</details>


### [153] [Do Agent Optimizers Compound? A Continual-Learning Evaluation on Terminal-Bench 2.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14004)
*Wenxiao Wang,Priyatham Kattakinda,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究智能体优化方法的收益是否能在连续任务中递归叠加。结果显示，只有将回归控制纳入优化循环的 RELAI-VCL 能在新任务上保持正迁移并持续提升，取得最高长期平均通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体优化方法通常只在固定基准上进行一次性优化，并将提升视为稳定效果；但真实部署场景中，新失败和新任务会不断出现，需要反复优化。因此，作者关注优化收益能否在多轮优化中持续叠加，同时不破坏先前获得的收益。

Method: 作者构建了一个基于 Terminal-Bench 2.0 困难任务的两阶段持续学习评估框架，在相同优化预算下比较三种 agent-harness 优化方法：GEPA、Meta Harness 和 RELAI 的 Verifiable Continual Learning（RELAI-VCL）。评估包括静态单阶段表现、新任务迁移表现，以及将新任务纳入目标后的第二轮优化表现。

Result: 三种方法在传统静态单阶段设置下均优于基线。但引入新任务后表现分化：GEPA 优化后的智能体迁移表现低于未优化基线；Meta Harness 迁移较好但第二轮优化后无法继续提升；RELAI-VCL 既能正向迁移到未见任务，又能在新任务纳入后继续提升。其长期平均通过率最高，为 76.4%，高于 GEPA 的 66.0%、Meta Harness 的 64.6% 和基线的 58.7%。

Conclusion: 优化收益只有在优化循环中显式加入回归控制时才会真正复合。回归控制为优化过程提供了避免捷径解和过拟合的归纳偏置，从而提升跨任务泛化能力并支持持续优化。

Abstract: Most reported gains from agent-optimization methods are one-shot: an agent is optimized against a fixed benchmark and the resulting improvement is reported as if it were a stable property of the method. This does not test the setting that matters for deployed agents, where optimization is applied recursively as new failures and new tasks appear over time. The central question this raises is whether optimizer-driven gains compound: after an agent has been optimized once, can it be optimized again on newly arrived tasks without eroding the gains the first round produced? We study this question with a two-phase continual-learning evaluation built from hard tasks in Terminal-Bench 2.0, comparing three approaches to agent-harness optimization (GEPA, Meta Harness, and RELAI's Verifiable Continual Learning, RELAI-VCL) under identical optimization budgets. All three methods improve over the baseline agent in the conventional, static, single-phase setting. However, once new tasks are introduced, the methods diverge sharply: GEPA's optimized agent transfers below the unoptimized baseline, Meta Harness transfers well but fails to improve further once given a second optimization budget, and RELAI-VCL is the only method that both transfers positively to unseen tasks and continues improving after those tasks are folded into the optimization objective, reaching the highest pass rate at every evaluated stage and the highest lifelong average pass rate overall (76.4% vs. 66.0% for GEPA, 64.6% for Meta Harness, and 58.7% for the baseline). Our key observation was that optimization gains compounded only when regression control was built into the optimization loop, providing an inductive bias against shortcut solutions that fail to generalize.

</details>


### [154] [AI-accelerated End-to-End Framework for Rapid Professional Upskilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14044)
*Tam Nguyen,Hung Nguyen,Robert Ogburn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个端到端AI加速的企业技能提升框架，覆盖知识获取、内容开发、审核验证、教学与评估开发五阶段，并通过外部认证、学习者考试结果和风险数据集产出展示行业有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 到2030年大量劳动者需要再培训或技能提升，而企业技能缺口弥合时间显著增加；现有框架多只优化单一环节，缺乏端到端能力与行业验证，因此需要一个同时提升内容生产效率与学习效率的完整方案。

Method: 构建覆盖五个阶段的AI加速框架：知识获取、课程内容开发、内容审查与验证、教学实施、评估开发；并围绕实际继续教育项目、专业认证备考与知识库下游分析进行外部验证。

Result: 基于该框架的技能提升项目获得继续专业教育学分审批；3名学习者在较短时间内完成学习并通过NVIDIA Agentic AI专业认证考试，另有14名学习者进行中；框架知识库还能支持复杂下游分析，例如生成包含1,267个风险项的多智能体AI系统风险管理数据集。

Conclusion: 该框架表明，AI可在企业技能提升项目的全流程中提高生产与学习效率，并具备一定行业认可和可迁移的知识资产价值；但样本规模仍较小，后续需更大规模、更多行业场景的验证。

Abstract: By 2030, 59 of every 100 workers will need reskilling or upskilling, yet the average time to close an enterprise skills gap grew from roughly 3 days in 2014 to 36 days in 2018. Most current frameworks accelerate single stages of upskilling programs and generally lack industry validation. We present an end-to-end framework that applies AI acceleration across five stages of knowledge acquisition, content development, content review and verification, teaching, and assessment development; with a strong focus on both production and learning efficiency. Three strong external signals validates the framework: the US National Association of State Boards of Accountancy reviewed and approved an upskilling program built on the framework for continuing-professional-education credits; 3 learners followed the program and passed the NVIDIA Certified Professional in Agentic AI exam in a significantly short amount of time, with 14 more in progress; the program's knowledge base supports complex downstream analysis such as the production of a robust 1,267 risk item dataset for managing multi-agent AI system risks.

</details>


### [155] [Earthquaker-AI: A Retrieval-Augmented Generation Framework with Rubric-Based Assessment for Primary School Earthquake Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14046)
*Xanthi Kokkinou,Chaido Mizeli,Nafsika Koulaxidou,Marina Delianidi,Konstantinos Diamantaras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Earthquaker-AI：将 Lego WeDo2 教育机器人与基于检索增强生成（RAG）的对话式 AI 助手结合，用于提升小学生的地震应急认知、行动能力与自我调节能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统教育机器人项目主要强调机械模拟和动手实践，但对学生在灾害情境中的认知加工、反思能力、语言表达和依据安全规范进行决策的支持不足。论文希望通过 AI 引导与分层评价，增强儿童对地震安全行动的理解与内化。

Method: 系统在原 Earthquaker STEM 项目基础上扩展：机器人部分使用 Lego WeDo2 的传感器和执行器模拟地震响应；AI 助手采用 RAG，将学生问题与官方安全指南进行语义匹配并生成回答；学习路径按年级递进，低年级用选择题和二维量规，中年级用动作序列识别和三轴量规，高年级用简短书面回答和四维量规评价。

Result: 实验评估显示，该系统回答具有较高的 groundedness 和准确性，幻觉率较低。系统能够提供基于量规的口头反馈，并支持学生在应急情境下保持冷静、进行自我调节和反思。

Conclusion: Earthquaker-AI 将动手式机器人学习、信息处理、量规评价和 RAG 对话 AI 融合，有助于提升小学生的技术素养、危机管理意识、自我调节能力以及负责任使用数字系统的能力。

Abstract: This paper presents Earthquaker-AI, a hybrid educational framework building upon a previously implemented educational robotics project by integrating a conversational AI assistant based on Retrieval-Augmented Generation. It aims to enhance earthquake preparedness and conscious action among primary-school students. The system extends the award-winning STEM project Earthquaker moving from mechanical simulation with Lego WeDo2 to cognitive and metacognitive processing. The robotics component uses Lego WeDo2 automation to simulate seismic response, letting students interact with sensors and actuators as tangible representations of protective actions. The assistant operates as a guided learning mechanism aligning student responses with safety guidelines, while providing rubric-based verbal feedback that supports self-regulated learning and calmness under emergency conditions. Earthquaker-AI follows a progressive learning trajectory aligned with cognitive development. In early grades, the focus is on basic recognition of safety actions through multiple-choice questions, assessed via a two-dimensional rubric. In middle grades, students identify correct action sequences through multiple-choice questions, evaluated via a three-axis rubric. In upper grades, the approach shifts to verbal production, requiring short written responses assessed via a four-dimensional rubric that includes clarity of expression. The dialogic module uses RAG to match student queries semantically with official guidelines, generating safe, accurate responses. Experimental evaluation shows high groundedness and accuracy, with a low hallucination rate. Overall, Earthquaker-AI combines hands-on engagement, information processing, and reflective practice. Combining robotics, rubrics, and AI promotes technological literacy, self-regulation, and responsible use of digital systems, contributing to early crisis-management skills.

</details>


### [156] [Deep Interaction: An Efficient Human-AI Interaction Method for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14049)
*Hefeng Zhou,Jinxuan Zhang,Jiong Lou,Yuxin Liu,Chaochao Lu,Jingjing Qu,Jie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 Deep Interaction：通过直接编辑原始 CoT 推理中的错误步骤，并将编辑后的推理蒸馏为提示，引导大模型沿纠正后的路径继续推理，从而提升纠错成功率并降低 token 消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CoT 纠错交互通常依赖重新生成答案，容易再次出错；或需要用户在后续对话中指出错误步骤，但模型可能表面承认错误后仍重复类似问题。作者希望提供一种更精确、高效、可保留正确推理步骤的人机干预机制。

Method: 提出 Deep Interaction 机制：允许用户直接编辑模型原始回答中的错误部分，同时保留正确推理链；随后将编辑后的 CoT 提炼/蒸馏为一个用于引导模型的 prompt，使模型沿修正后的推理路径生成后续答案。

Result: 在 STEM 推理任务上，相比基线方法，该方法将纠错成功率提升超过 25%，同时 token 使用量约减少 40%。

Conclusion: 直接编辑并蒸馏修正后的 CoT 是一种有效的人机协同纠错方式，能够提高复杂推理任务中的纠错可靠性和交互效率。

Abstract: The emergence of Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly enhanced the ability of large language models (LLMs) to tackle complex, multi-step tasks. However, when errors occur, current interaction approaches typically involve re-generating another response that may make mistakes again, or users laboriously flag the faulty step in follow-up turns that may get responses <You are right, I made a mistake here> followed by similar errors recurring. To address this issue, we propose an efficient human intervention mechanism for precisely correcting reasoning errors in LLMs, termed Deep Interaction. Our approach enables direct editing of the original response, allowing erroneous parts to be corrected while preserving accurate reasoning steps. We refine the edited CoT into a distilled prompt, which then steers the LLM along the corrected reasoning path. Experimental results show that our method achieves over a 25% improvement in correction success rate and reduces token usage by approximately 40% on STEM tasks reasoning compared to baseline approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Mixed-Timescale Differential Coding for Downlink Model Broadcast in Wireless Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13119)
*Chung-Hsuan Hu,Zheng Chen,Erik G. Larsson*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文提出混合时间尺度差分编码（MTDC），用于联邦学习下行全局模型广播，在链路失败时仍能帮助设备重构最新模型，并在相近通信预算下提升学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习每轮向设备广播完整全局模型，通信开销高。利用连续全局模型的时间相关性可用差分编码降低传输量和量化比特，但无线下行失败会导致设备漏接差分更新，无法重构最新模型，从而使用过期模型训练或空闲等待。

Method: 提出MTDC方案，在两个不同层级进行差分编码，通过调整参考模型实现混合时间尺度更新；即使设备漏接某次差分更新，也可在两次完整模型广播之间重构最新全局模型。作者还给出收敛分析，并据此设计年龄感知的MTDC变体和设备调度策略，以提高通信效率。

Result: 仿真表明，在存在下行传输失败且通信资源预算相近的条件下，MTDC及其变体相较基线方法取得更优学习性能。

Conclusion: MTDC有效缓解了差分编码在不可靠无线下行中的失步问题，在保证通信效率的同时改善模型训练表现，适合资源受限且链路不稳定的联邦学习场景。

Abstract: In standard federated learning systems, the parameter server broadcasts the global model to the participating devices in every iteration. Motivated by the temporal correlation between consecutive global models, differential coding can be applied to global model dissemination to reduce the information magnitude, thereby enabling communication with fewer quantization bits. However, due to wireless link failures, devices may occasionally miss differential updates and consequently fail to reconstruct the global model. As a result, they either continue local training based on an outdated model or remain idle until the next full-model broadcast becomes available. To address this challenge, we propose a mixed-timescale differential coding (MTDC) scheme that performs differential coding at two different levels by adjusting the reference model. With MTDC, a device can reconstruct the latest global model between two full-model broadcasts even if it misses a differential update. We provide a convergence analysis that motivates the design of an age-aware variant of MTDC, along with a device scheduling policy to further improve communication efficiency. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed MTDC schemes achieve superior learning performance compared to baseline methods under similar communication resource budgets in the presence of downlink transmission failures.

</details>


### [158] [Active Beyond-Diagonal RIS Empowered Heterogeneous Edge Computing: A Distributional Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13160)
*Tianyu Pang,Hongyu Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究互易有源BD-RIS辅助异构MEC系统中的能量感知卸载与资源分配，并提出DSAC-T端到端联合优化框架以提升能耗-时延性能与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有源BD-RIS可通过混合透射/反射实现信号放大和全空间覆盖，适合解决MEC上行卸载中的遮挡问题；但实际互易器件会产生跨扇区能量泄漏，改变系统能量-时延权衡，使传统优化难以高效处理。

Method: 将卸载决策、CPU/GPU计算资源分配、发射功率、接收处理和有源BD-RIS配置联合建模为高维混合整数非凸问题；提出基于分布式软演员-评论家算法改进版DSAC-T的端到端学习框架，通过建模回报分布而非仅期望回报来增强策略稳定性。

Result: 相较基线算法，DSAC-T取得最佳能量-时延奖励、最高81.67%的可行性比例，并实现每个场景0.0267秒的快速在线决策。

Conclusion: DSAC-T能有效处理互易有源BD-RIS辅助异构MEC中的复杂耦合资源优化问题，在性能、可行性和在线推理速度之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Active beyond-diagonal reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (BD-RISs) enables hybrid transmitting and reflecting mode to achieve effective signal amplification and full-space coverage, thus providing a promising solution for blockage-aware uplink offloading in heterogeneous mobile edge computing (MEC) systems. However, practical hybrid mode active BD-RIS are realized by reciprocal devices, which inherently generate cross-sector energy leakage that will reshape the system-level energy-latency tradeoff. This paper studies energy-aware offloading and resource allocation for reciprocal active BD-RIS-assisted heterogeneous MEC, where offloading decisions, CPU/GPU computation allocation, transmit powers, receive processing, and active BD-RIS are tightly coupled. The resulting problem is a high-dimensional mixed integer nonconvex problem and is difficult to solve efficiently by conventional per-instance optimization. To address this challenge, we develop an end-to-end joint optimization framework based on a refined version of the distributional soft actor--critic algorithm, named as DSAC-T. By modeling return distributions rather than only expected values, DSAC-T improves policy stability under reward heterogeneity and feasibility-boundary sensitivity. Compared with other baseline algorithms, DSAC-T achieves the best energy-latency reward, the highest feasibility ratio of 81.67%, and a fast online decision time of 0.0267 s per scenario.

</details>


### [159] [Construction of Generalized Weighing-Hadamard Matrices over Finite Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13324)
*Gustavo T. Bastos,Miguel Beltrá,Sara D. Cardell,Verónica Requena*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上的广义称重-Hadamard（GWH）矩阵，重点关注其可逆子集的代数结构、正交等价关系及其在编码理论中的潜在应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GWH矩阵在组合设计、有限域矩阵理论和编码理论中具有重要作用。明确其存在性、构造方法及等价分类，有助于理解其结构并推动其在编码理论中的应用。

Method: 作者研究GWH矩阵的若干性质与构造，特别考察可逆GWH矩阵在矩阵乘法下的封闭性；引入由正交变换定义的强等价概念，并分析由正交矩阵子群诱导的商群结构。

Result: 论文证明可逆GWH矩阵在矩阵乘法下构成一个群；进一步证明按正交矩阵子群取商后得到的商群是阿贝尔群；同时讨论了这些矩阵在编码理论中的应用。

Conclusion: 该工作从群论和等价分类角度深化了有限域上GWH矩阵的理论基础，并为其在编码理论中的构造和分析提供了新的代数工具。

Abstract: The existence, several properties, and constructions of Generalized Weighing-Hadamard (GWH) matrices over finite fields are addressed in this work. We study the subset of invertible GWH matrices and show that it forms a group under matrix multiplication. Besides that, we introduce a strong notion of equivalence between such matrices, defined via orthogonal transformations, and further prove that the corresponding quotient group by the subgroup of orthogonal matrices is abelian. Finally, we discuss some applications of these matrices in coding theory

</details>


### [160] [$r$-Minimal Poset Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13520)
*Yang Xu,Haibin Kan,Guangyue Han*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究偏序支撑下的 r-极小码，建立其与 cutting r-blocking maps/sets 的等价刻画，并推广 Ashikhmin–Barg 判据，给出存在性结果及分层偏序情形的结构刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hamming 度量下的极小码已有丰富研究，但在更一般的偏序支撑与加权偏序度量框架中，r-极小性的理论刻画、判别准则和构造存在性仍需系统推广。

Method: 作者在坐标集上引入偏序 P，并定义 P-support 下的 r-极小码；通过 cutting r-blocking maps 建立几何/组合等价刻画；进一步利用任意正权函数诱导的 (P,ω)-weight 给出充要判据；分别讨论一般偏序、链的不交并以及分层偏序等特殊结构。

Result: 论文给出了 r-极小 P-码与 cutting r-blocking maps 的等价刻画，提出了基于 (P,ω)-权重的 r-极小性充要条件，从而推广 Hamming 情形的 Ashikhmin–Barg 准则；证明了两类存在性结果；在分层偏序下，将 r-极小 P-码刻画为与 r-极小 Hamming 码相关的结构；并刻画了两层分层偏序诱导的 cutting r-blocking sets。

Conclusion: 该工作将极小码理论从 Hamming 度量系统扩展到偏序支撑和加权偏序度量环境，统一并推广了已有判据与阻塞集解释，同时为特定偏序结构下的构造和分类提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose and study $r$-minimal codes with respect to $\mathbf{P}$-support, where $\mathbf{P}=(Ω,\preccurlyeq_{\mathbf{P}})$ is a poset defined on the coordinate set of the ambient space $\mathbf{H}$. $r$-Minimal $\mathbf{P}$-codes are natural extensions of Hamming metric minimal codes that have been extensively studied in the literature. We characterize $r$-minimal $\mathbf{P}$-codes in terms of the notion so called cutting $r$-blocking maps, which generalizes the well-known equivalence between minimal Hamming metric codes and cutting blocking sets. We also give a necessary and sufficient condition for $r$-minimality in terms of $(\mathbf{P},ω)$-weight defined on $\mathbf{H}$, where $ω:Ω\longrightarrow\mathbb{R}^{+}$ is an arbitrary weight function. This leads to a generalization of the well-known Ashikhmin-Barg criterion for Hamming metric minimal codes. We then prove two existence results for $r$-minimal $\mathbf{P}$-codes, both for general $\mathbf{P}$ and for the special case that $\mathbf{P}$ is a disjoint union of chains. When $\mathbf{P}$ is hierarchical, we characterize $r$-minimal $\mathbf{P}$-codes in terms of $r$-minimal Hamming metric codes. Finally, we characterize cutting $r$-blocking sets induced by hierarchical posets with two levels, which further enables us to answer a question raised in Hyun, Kim, Wu and Yue \cite{28}.

</details>


### [161] [Optimal Non-Binary Single-Track Gray Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13588)
*Tuvi Etzion*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在 F_3 和 F_5 上存在长度为 p^t、包含 p^{p^t} 个码字的单轨 Gray 码，并说明更大素数情形可由 t=2 的构造推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轨 Gray 码在量化与编码应用中相比传统 Gray 码具有优势，但除 n=2 外不存在包含全部 2^n 个二进制码字的单轨二进制 Gray 码，因此需要研究在更大字母表（尤其有限域）上的存在性。

Method: 论文从组合编码理论角度研究循环 Gray 码，针对长度为 p^t、字母表为有限域 F_p 的单轨 Gray 码构造或证明其存在性，并分析小素数 p=3、5 及更大素数在 t=2 情形下到任意 t>2 的推广关系。

Result: 证明了当 p=3 和 p=5 时，存在长度为 p^t（n≥2）、码字数为 p^{p^t}、定义在 F_p 上的单轨 Gray 码。对于更大素数 p，若 t=2 时存在合适代码，则可推出任意 t>2 时也存在相应代码；同时给出非素数字母表大小 m 下此类代码也可能存在的迹象。

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了单轨 Gray 码的存在性结果，表明在三元和五元有限域上可以构造规模完整的单轨 Gray 码，并为更大素数及非素数字母表情形提供了进一步存在性的理论依据。

Abstract: A single-track Gray code is a cyclic Gray code with codewords of length $n$, over an alphabet of size $m$, such that all the $n$ tracks that correspond to the $n$ distinct coordinates of the codewords are cyclic shifts of the first track. Such codes have advantages over the conventional Gray codes in certain quantization and coding applications. Unless $n=2$, there are no such binary codes that contain all the $2^n$ codewords of length $n$. In this paper, we prove that such codes of length $p^t$, $n \geq 2$, with $p^{p^t}$ codewords, over $\F_p$, $p$ prime, exist, for $p=3$ and $p=5$. For larger prime $p$ an appropriate code for $t=2$, implies the existence of such a code for any $t >2$. If the alphabet size $m$ is not a prime there are also indications that such codes exist.

</details>


### [162] [Low-Complexity Soft-Aided Error-and-Erasure Decoding for Generalized Product Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13719)
*Sisi Miao,Laurent Schmalen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低复杂度的GPC迭代译码器RDRSD，通过动态可靠性评分在接近iBDD复杂度下获得约1 dB增益，并用软辅助后处理降低误码地板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义乘积码（GPCs）具备优异的高码率性能和低复杂度硬件实现潜力，但传统硬判决迭代BDD（iBDD）在性能上存在局限，尤其在小纠错能力组件码场景下可能出现较明显的误码地板。

Method: 提出精化动态可靠性评分译码器（RDRSD），一种基于硬消息传递的迭代纠错与擦除译码方法，利用动态可靠性分数指导译码；同时给出综合域实现以保持接近iBDD的复杂度，并针对小纠错能力组件码分析误码地板，设计软辅助后处理步骤。

Result: 在多种GPC及译码配置下，RDRSD提供了不同的复杂度—性能折中；其复杂度与硬判决iBDD相当，同时相较iBDD约获得1 dB编码增益。软辅助后处理能显著降低小纠错能力组件码的误码地板。

Conclusion: RDRSD在保持低复杂度、适合硬件实现的同时，明显提升了GPC译码性能；结合软辅助后处理后，还能缓解特定GPC配置中的误码地板问题。

Abstract: Generalized product codes (GPCs) combine excellent high-rate performance with low-complexity hardware implementations. We propose the refined dynamic reliability score decoder (\acs{RDRSD}), a hard-message-passing iterative error-and-erasure decoder that uses dynamic reliability scores. Its syndrome-domain implementation has complexity comparable to hard-decision \ac{iBDD}. Across various GPCs and decoding configurations, \acs{RDRSD} provides different complexity--performance trade-offs and achieves approximately \SI{1}{dB} coding gain over \ac{iBDD}. For GPCs with component codes of small error-correcting capability, we also analyze the error floor and propose a soft-aided post-processing step that significantly lowers it.

</details>


### [163] [Precoding-based protocols for entanglement assisted linear computation over a quantum many-to-one network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13756)
*Ruoyu Meng,Aditya Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究无噪声量子多对一网络中线性组合的计算问题，提出更一般的量子编码/预编码协议，在若干情形下降低通信成本，并证明联合计算多个线性函数可产生次可加增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 N-Sum Box 协议能够在特定约束下计算发送端分布数据的线性组合，但其适用的线性变换类别和数据分布条件有限。本文旨在放宽这些限制，支持更一般的线性变换，同时保持或降低通信开销。

Method: 在包含 k 个发送者和一个接收者的无噪声量子多对一网络中，发送者共享纠缠但不与接收者共享纠缠，也不能彼此通信。作者为发送者提供更多 qudit，并允许其对输入符号进行精心预编码，将经典向量信息编码到本地量子子系统后发送给接收者，由接收者通过量子测量和后处理恢复目标线性组合。

Result: 所提出协议可计算比既有 N-Sum Box 类方法更广泛的线性变换；其通信成本不高于当前最佳已知结果，并在某些实例中严格更低。作者还给出实例表明通信成本在多个计算任务联合执行时具有次可加性。

Conclusion: 通过增加发送端可用量子资源并引入预编码，量子多对一网络中的线性函数计算可以获得更强表达能力和更优通信效率；此外，联合计算线性函数可能比逐个计算更节省通信资源。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the problem of computing a linear combination over a noiseless quantum many-to-one network. There are $k$ senders, Alice$_1$, $\ldots$, Alice$_k$, and a single receiver, Bob. Each Alice$_i$ has a data vector $W_i \in \mathbb{F}^{m_i}$, where $\mathbb{F}$ is a finite field. Bob wants to compute the linear combination $Y = V_1 W_1 + V_2 W_2 + \cdots + V_k W_k \in \mathbb{F}^m$, where $V_i$ is an $m \times m_i$ matrix over $\mathbb{F}$. The senders transmit quantum states to Bob through a noiseless many-to-one quantum network, but they are not allowed to communicate with each other. The senders share entanglement among themselves, while Bob does not share this entanglement. They encode their classical information $W_i$, $i=1,\ldots,k$, into their local subsystems and transmit them to Bob so that he can recover $Y$ through a quantum measurement and subsequent post-processing. The N-Sum Box protocol proposed by Allaix et al. (2025) considers this problem under certain constraints on the linear combination and the distribution of the data vectors among the senders.
  We present protocols that support the computation of a more general class of linear transformations by giving the senders access to more qudits and allowing them to judiciously precode their input symbols. The communication cost of our schemes is at most that of the best-known prior results in this area and is strictly lower in certain cases. Finally, we demonstrate that the communication cost is subadditive across instances. Specifically, we identify two linear functions for which the total cost of computing them individually is strictly larger than the cost of computing them jointly.

</details>


### [164] [CAS I: A Geometric Coding Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13796)
*Romie Banerjee*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文将经典编码定理推广到对称群框架，提出“对称先验”，并证明在 fix-retractable 对称群上可得到通用下半可计算半测度及几何编码定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典算法信息论中的编码定理建立了复杂度与通用先验之间的联系，但其结构通常基于程序或机器模型。本文希望从群作用与可计算双射的角度重新刻画复杂度，为由对称性诱导的信息度量建立理论基础。

Method: 作者将二进制串集合上的可计算双射称为对称，定义某群中随机选取的对称以某字符串为唯一不动点的概率为该字符串的对称先验。随后引入 fix-retractable 对称群，即存在可计算截面为每个字符串选择隔离对称的群，并在此条件下证明对称先验的半测度性质与通用性。同时构造子群与字符串子集之间的 Galois 连接，分析闭点、极大闭子群以及稠密子群的并半格结构。

Result: 论文证明：对任意 fix-retractable 对称群，对称先验是通用下半可计算半测度，并由此得到几何编码定理。此外，论文给出了相关 Galois 连接的结构性刻画，并讨论了稠密子群的代数组织方式。

Conclusion: 该工作把算法信息论与群论统一在一个对称性框架中，提出了研究对称诱导复杂度的新路径，并作为 Computational Algorithmic Statistics 系列的开篇，为后续理论发展奠定基础。

Abstract: This paper establishes a direct analogue of the classical Coding Theorem in the setting of symmetry groups. We consider computable bijections on the set of binary strings, called symmetries and define the symmetry prior of a string as the probability that a randomly chosen symmetry from a given group has the string as its unique fixed point. We show that for any fix-retractable symmetry group, a group admitting a computable section that selects an isolating symmetry for every string, the symmetry prior is a universal lower semi-computable semi-measure. In this case, the Geometric Coding Theorem holds.
  We also develop a Galois connection between subgroups of G and subsets of binary strings, characterizing closed points and maximal closed subgroups, and explore the join-semilattice of dense subgroups. Our results unify algorithmic information theory with group theory and provide a framework for studying symmetry-induced complexity measures.
  This paper is the first in a series on Computational Algorithmic Statistics (CAS).

</details>


### [165] [Safety-Aware Forward Detection in Networked ISAC for Low-Altitude UAV Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13908)
*Jingli Li,Yiyan Ma,Wei Chen,Weijie Yuan,Qingqing Cheng,Tongyang Xu,Guoyu Ma,Mi Yang,Yunlong Lu,Wenwei Yue,Zhangdui Zhong*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向低空无线网络中无人机安全飞行的网络化ISAC前向检测设计，通过多地面基站协同同时支持下行通信、无人机状态估计与前向区域非合作目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络化ISAC研究多关注通信增强或目标参数估计，对无人机前向区域内非合作目标的检测可靠性研究不足，而该能力直接关系到低空飞行安全。

Method: 根据无人机位置、速度和安全制动距离确定前向感兴趣区域并体素化；推导无人机状态估计CRLB与前向区域漏检概率，并分析其随协作基站数的缩放规律；进一步联合优化感知导频比例、发射功率和波束方向，在通信速率约束下平衡状态估计与前向检测性能。

Result: 理论上，状态估计CRLB近似随协作基站数J按ln^{-2}J下降，前向区域漏检概率呈与λ_t D_f ln^{-2}J相关的指数型缩放规律。仿真显示，相比无前向检测基线，平均漏检概率及相应感知导致的碰撞风险降低17.05%，平均CRLB增加14.82%。

Conclusion: 该设计能在仅带来有限状态估计性能损失的情况下显著提升前向非合作目标检测可靠性，为低空网络中安全感知通信一体化资源配置提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Networked integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) exploits cooperation among multiple ground base stations (GBSs) to support safe uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) flight in low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs). Existing studies mainly focus on communication enhancement or target parameter estimation, while the detection reliability of non-cooperative targets in the UAV forward region remains insufficiently investigated. To address this issue, this paper proposes a safety-aware forward detection design in networked ISAC, where multiple GBSs jointly support UAV downlink communication, state estimation, and non-cooperative target detection within the forward region of interest (ROI). First, the forward ROI is determined by the UAV position, velocity, and safe braking distance, and is voxelized to characterize target-existence states. Then, the Cramér-Rao lower bound (CRLB) for UAV state estimation and the forward-ROI miss-detection probability are derived, and their scaling laws are characterized: In detail, the UAV state-estimation CRLB approximately decreases as $\ln^{-2}J$ with the number of cooperative GBSs $J$, while the forward-ROI miss-detection probability follows an exponential-form scaling law as $λ_{t}D_{f}\ln^{-2}J$. Furthermore, a safety-aware resource optimization problem is formulated to jointly configure the sensing pilot ratio, transmit power, and beam direction, balancing UAV state-estimation performance and forward detection reliability under the communication-rate constraint. Simulation results show that, compared with the baseline scheme without forward detection, the proposed design reduces the average miss-detection probability and the corresponding sensing-induced collision risk by $17.05\%$, while introducing only limited state-estimation performance degradation, reflected by a $14.82\%$ increase in the average CRLB.

</details>


### [166] [Square-Root Law for Covert Communication with Warden-Favorable Side Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14013)
*Hossein Ahmadi,Christian Deppe,Boulat A. Bash,Eduard A. Jorswieck*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究高斯隐蔽叠加通信模型，证明在Willie扣除公开分量后的残差满足高斯且方差已知时，隐蔽通信仍遵循平方根定律，其一阶常数由Willie实际检测输入残差方差决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐蔽通信要求Alice向Bob传输信息的同时让Willie难以检测。已有平方根定律常以Willie接收噪声为核心，但在叠加公开信号并进行后验抵消的实际场景中，Willie检测的是抵消后的残差，因此需要刻画残差方差、抵消误差和边信息对隐蔽容量一阶常数的影响。

Method: 论文建立标量高斯covert-overlay模型：Alice的低功率隐蔽信号叠加在公开分量上，Willie拥有协议、时序、导频、信道估计和校准等可获得边信息，并先对公开分量作最佳估计和抵消；随后对条件残差施加相对熵隐蔽约束。作者在平稳残差方差情形推导可可靠传输载荷的一阶表达式，并通过低功率高斯信号实现性与匹配反证明最优性；同时扩展到已知时变残差方差并给出功率分配规则。

Result: 在平稳情形下，无隐蔽传输时Willie检测输入残差方差为σ0²=σW²+σe²，其中σW²为Willie接收噪声方差，σe²为不可消除的公开分量抵消误差。n次信道使用下最大隐蔽载荷为RC*√n(1+o(1))比特，RC*=σ0²/(σB² ln2)·√δ。低功率高斯信号达到该常数，反证明表明其在条件加性高斯创新模型内一阶最优。时变情形中，应向残差方差更大的时隙分配更多隐蔽功率。

Conclusion: 论文表明，隐蔽通信平方根定律的一阶常数由Willie实际检测输入的后抵消残差方差决定，而不是单纯由Willie接收机噪声决定。该结论适用于高斯、条件方差已知的后抵消残差模型；非高斯残差以及固定不消失的方差不确定性不在本文范围内。

Abstract: Covert communication enables Alice to transmit to Bob while making the transmission difficult for Willie to detect. We study a scalar Gaussian covert-overlay model in which Alice's low-power covert signal is superimposed on an aggregate public component generated by Alice or other trackable sources. Willie is given all physically obtainable side information, including protocol details, timing, pilots, channel estimates, and calibration information, and subtracts his best estimate of the public component before testing. Covertness is imposed on the resulting residual through a relative-entropy constraint with budget $δ$ conditioned on Willie's side information. In the stationary case, the residual under no covert transmission has variance $σ_0^2=σ_W^2+σ_e^2$, where $σ_W^2$ is Willie's receiver-noise variance and $σ_e^2$ is the irreducible cancellation error. Over $n$ channel uses, the maximal reliably transmissible covert payload is $R_C^\star\sqrt{n}(1+o(1))$ bits, where $R_C^\star=\frac{σ_0^2}{σ_B^2\ln 2}\sqrtδ$, and $σ_B^2$ is Bob's receiver-noise variance. Thus, the square-root-law (SRL) constant is governed by the variance at Willie's actual detector input, not by receiver noise alone. Low-power Gaussian signaling achieves this constant, and a matching converse establishes first-order optimality within the conditioned additive Gaussian innovation model. For known time-varying conditioned residual variances, we also derive the first-order allocation, which assigns more covert power to larger residual variances. The results require a Gaussian post-cancellation null residual with known conditioned variance; non-Gaussian residuals and fixed non-vanishing variance uncertainty are outside the scope of this paper.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Prospective clinical indication, post-hoc report leakage, and fusion design in multi-image chest radiograph classification: a patient-clustered evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13800)
*Kamran Shahid,Muhammad Munwar Iqbal*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文评估胸片研究中不同输入来源（图像、临床适应证、报告文本）对报告派生标签预测的影响，发现临床适应证信息贡献显著，置换感知的多模态融合具有竞争力，而使用完整报告文本会造成严重标签泄漏/循环性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸片数据集常将影像、Clinical Indication、Findings 和 Impression 混合作为输入，但这些信息产生于诊疗流程的不同阶段。若将检查后生成的报告文本用于预测由报告派生的标签，可能导致信息泄漏和不真实的性能估计。研究旨在区分前瞻可用信息与事后文本信息的作用。

Method: 研究使用15,000个ReXGradient-160K研究，每例含两张可读图像和五个由CheXbert派生的报告观察标签。采用冻结的DenseNet-121图像编码器和Bio+ClinicalBERT文本编码器，比较图像-only、Indication-only、固定顺序多模态融合、随机交换、DeepSets和SectionGuard-MI等模型；Findings和Impression仅作为事后泄漏控制。训练使用五个随机种子，并通过2,000次患者聚类bootstrap估计公开测试集不确定性。

Result: 在U-Ones设置下，主图像macro AUROC为0.643，两图像为0.694，Indication为0.749，普通两图像+Indication融合为0.780。SectionGuard-MI的AUROC为0.783、AUPRC为0.260；相对普通融合，AUROC差异0.0031且不显著，AUPRC差异0.0289且显著。DeepSets获得最高前瞻AUROC点估计0.787，random-swap融合获得最高前瞻AUPRC点估计0.265并且校准优于SectionGuard-MI。完整报告文本单独预测达到AUROC 0.979、AUPRC 0.836，即便经过精确或扩展遮蔽AUROC仍高于0.973。

Conclusion: 研究表明，前瞻可获得的Clinical Indication与报告派生标签高度相关；考虑图像顺序/置换不变性的融合策略具有竞争力；而使用Findings、Impression或完整报告文本会引入显著报告-标签循环性，导致对模型性能的高估。

Abstract: Chest radiograph datasets often combine multiple images with Clinical Indication, Findings, and Impression, although these inputs are produced at different stages of care. We evaluated 15,000 ReXGradient-160K studies with two readable images and five CheXbert-derived report observations. Frozen DenseNet-121 and Bio+ClinicalBERT encoders were used to compare image-only, Indication-only, fixed-order multimodal, random-swap, DeepSets, and SectionGuard-MI models. Findings and Impression were evaluated only as post-hoc leakage controls. Models were trained with five seeds, and public-test uncertainty was estimated with 2,000 patient-cluster bootstrap replicates. Under U-Ones, macro AUROC was 0.643 for the primary image, 0.694 for two images, 0.749 for Indication, and 0.780 for ordinary two-image-plus-Indication fusion. SectionGuard-MI achieved AUROC 0.783 and AUPRC 0.260. Relative to ordinary fusion, its paired AUROC difference was 0.0031 (95% CI, -0.0042 to 0.0104; adjusted p=0.374), while its AUPRC difference was 0.0289 (95% CI, 0.0095 to 0.0413; adjusted p=0.004). DeepSets had the highest prospective AUROC point estimate (0.787), and random-swap fusion had the highest prospective AUPRC point estimate (0.265) with better calibration than SectionGuard-MI. Full report text alone reached AUROC 0.979 and AUPRC 0.836; AUROC remained above 0.973 after exact or expanded masking. These results show that prospective Indication is strongly associated with report-derived targets, permutation-aware fusion is competitive, and post-hoc report text creates substantial report-label circularity.

</details>
